{"batchcomplete":true,"query":{"normalized":[{"fromencoded":false,"from":"List_of_UFC_events","to":"List of UFC events"}],"pages":[{"pageid":28418256,"ns":0,"title":"List of UFC events","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{Short description|none}}\n{{use mdy dates|date=July 2021}}\n{{Use American English|date=April 2023}}\n[[Image:UFC 74 Respect Bout.jpg|right|300px|thumb|All UFC bouts take place inside [[Mixed martial arts cage|the Octagon]].]]\n\n==Overview==\nThe UFC's [[mixed martial arts]] (MMA) events are separated into nine different formats:\n*\"Numbered\" events, currently airing on [[Paramount+]] and formerly aired on [[pay-per-view]], with some exceptions.\n*\"Fight Night\" events, currently airing on [[Paramount+]] and formerly aired on [[Spike (TV network)|Spike]], [[Fox Sports 1]], [[Fox Sports 2]], [[UFC Fight Pass]] or [[ESPN+]]. \n*\"The Ultimate Fighter Finale\" events, formerly aired on Spike, [[FX (TV channel)|FX]], Fox Sports 1, UFC Fight Pass or ESPN+.\n*\"UFC Live\" events, formerly aired on [[Versus (TV channel)|Versus]]\n*\"[[Fox UFC|UFC on Fox]]\" events, formerly aired on [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]]\n*\"UFC on FX\" events, formerly aired on [[FX (TV channel)|FX]]\n*\"UFC on Fuel TV\" events, formerly aired on [[Fox Sports 2|Fuel TV]]\n*\"UFC on ESPN\" events, formerly aired on [[ESPN]] or [[ESPN2]]\n*\"UFC on ABC\" events, formerly aired on [[American Broadcasting Company|ABC]]\n\nMost of the \"numbered\" events have taken place on pay-per-view, though there have been a few exceptions for reasons such as [[Broadcast delay|tape-delay]]. Events such as [[UFC 72]], which took place in [[Belfast]], [[Northern Ireland]], were sold on pay-per-view, but due to tape-delay, purchase rates were not as high as events that aired at a traditional starting time.{{cite web|url=http://mmapayout.com/2010/09/who-is-the-biggest-ufc-ppv-draw-part-5/|title=Who is the biggest UFC PPV draw part 5|publisher=mmapayout.com|date=September 8, 2010|access-date=October 20, 2010|archive-date=April 5, 2023|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230405015847/http://mmapayout.com/2010/09/who-is-the-biggest-ufc-ppv-draw-part-5/|url-status=dead}} As such, events taking part in significantly different time zones, including [[UFC 70]], [[UFC 75]], [[UFC 89]], [[UFC 95]], [[UFC 105]], [[UFC 120]], [[UFC 122]], and [[UFC 138]], were given a same-night airing in American [[prime time]] on Spike.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/20995/spike-tv-announces-8-p-m-etpt-start-time-for-saturdays-ufc-120-event.mma|title=Spike TV announces 8p.m. ET/PT start time for Saturday's UFC 120 event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=October 13, 2010|access-date=October 20, 2010|archive-date=April 5, 2023|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230405015847/http://mmajunkie.com/news/20995/spike-tv-announces-8-p-m-etpt-start-time-for-saturdays-ufc-120-event.mma|url-status=dead}}\n\nHistorically, the UFC has also aired preliminary fights prior to the main fight cards, either on television via Spike, FX, and Fuel TV, or online via [[Facebook]] and [[YouTube]]. Beginning in August 2013, starting with [[UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Sonnen]], the UFC began airing non-PPV events on either [[Fox Sports 1]] or [[Fox Sports 2]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-fight-night-27-condit-vs-kampmann-2-lineup-set-with-fox-sports-2-prelims|title='UFC Fight Night 27: Condit vs. Kampmann 2' lineup set with FOX Sports 2 prelims|date=July 19, 2013}} In January 2014, as part of the organization's global expansion, the UFC began airing various international events exclusively on their subscription-based digital network, UFC Fight Pass.{{cite web|url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2013/12/02/ufc-to-unveil-digital-network-for-global-expansion-/3797951/|title=UFC to unveil digital network for global expansion|publisher=USAtoday.com|author=Staff|date=2013-12-02|access-date=2013-12-02}}\n\nIn July 2009, the UFC held its unofficial 100th \"numbered event\"; however, officially, despite being called UFC 100, it was actually the 101st numbered event (not to mention the 133rd event overall). The UFC signed a deal with [[Fox Sports Networks|Fox Sports Net]], after Fox agreed to broadcast one fight in June 2002, during its \"All-Star Summer\" that month.{{cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/news?slug=ki-ufcthirtyseven050909|title=UFC 37 (And 37.5, too)|date=May 9, 2009 |publisher=sports.yahoo.com|access-date=October 13, 2010}} UFC 37 and 38 had already been scheduled and promoted, but as UFC 38 was the promotion's debut in the United Kingdom, they created UFC 37.5; an event that featured the first ever mixed martial arts fight available on cable television.\n\nAs of [[UFC 334|UFC 334: Gane vs. Hokit]], which took place on November 14, 2026, there have been 798 UFC events held in 170 cities in 33 countries.{{cite web|access-date=2026-08-01 |author=E. Spencer Kyte |date=2026-07-28 |language=en |publisher=ufc.com |title=Fight By Fight Preview {{!}} UFC Belgrade |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/fight-by-fight-preview-ufc-belgrade-serbia}}\n\n===Other events===\nIn May 2017, the UFC announced [[Dana White]] would hold [[Dana White's Tuesday Night Contender Series]] weekly on [[UFC Fight Pass]] (becoming, from January 2019 on, exclusive content on ESPN+ in the United States). As with the earlier web series ''Looking for a Fight'', the goal of the series is for White to scout talent for the UFC.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/dana-white-tuesday-night-contender-series-launches-on-fight-pass-051217?id=|title=Dana White's Tuesday Night Contender Series Launches on Fight Pass|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|author=Staff|access-date=2017-06-22|date=2017-05-12}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/dana-whites-tuesday-night-contender-series-debuts-july-11-on-ufc-fight-pass-with-5-live-fights-a-week|title='Dana White's Tuesday Night Contender Series' debuts July 11 on UFC Fight Pass with 5 live fights a week|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2017-05-13|date=2017-05-13}} It was stated ahead of the Tuesday Night Contender Series' license being approved that “this is not the UFC, this is not the UFC brand,” but instead a promotion that will allow up and coming fighters the chance to showcase their talents in hopes that one day they may compete in the UFC.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/dana-white-receives-promoters-license-for-new-contender-series-separate-from-ufc-061417|title=Dana White receives promoter's license for new contender series separate from UFC|date=14 June 2017|access-date=20 February 2018}}\n\nIn June 2025, the UFC launched [[UFC BJJ|UFC Brazilian Jiu-Jitsu]] (UFC BJJ), a sister [[Brazilian Jiu-Jitsu]] organization.{{cite web |date=June 6, 2025 |title=UFC Announces Launch of UFC Brazilian Jiu‑Jitsu, World’s New Premier Brazilian Jiu‑Jitsu Live Event Series |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-announces-launch-ufc-brazilian-jiu-jitsu-worlds-new-premier-brazilian-jiu-jitsu-live-event |access-date=July 9, 2025 |website=UFC.com |publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship}}{{cite web |author=Adam Stern |date=June 23, 2025 |title=With launch of BJJ property, UFC aims to deepen combat sports moat |url=https://www.sportsbusinessjournal.com/Articles/2025/06/23/with-launch-of-bjj-property-ufc-aims-to-deepen-combat-sports-moat/ |access-date=July 9, 2025 |website=Sports Business Journal}} Unlike standard UFC events which feature MMA fights held inside the [[Mixed martial arts cage|octagon cage]], [[UFC BJJ#List of events|UFC BJJ events]] feature [[submission grappling]] matches inside an octagonal \"bowl\".{{Cite web |title=UFC BJJ Rules and Scoring |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-bjj-rules-and-scoring |website=UFC.com |publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship |access-date=July 9, 2025}}\n\n==Scheduled events==\n{| id=\"Scheduled events\" class=\"sortable wikitable succession-box\" style=\"font-size:90%; \"\n! scope=\"col\" | Event\n! scope=\"col\" | Date\n! scope=\"col\" |
